<script type="text/javascript">
    var classDropdownAccountMenuContent = document.getElementsByClassName('dropdown-account-menu-content');
    var divMaskForDropdownAccountMenu = document.getElementById("divMaskForDropdownAccountMenu");
    var IsVisibleAccountMenu = false;
    function ShowAccountMenu(el) {
        if (!IsVisibleAccountMenu) {
            window.addEventListener('click', varBodyDocumentClickListener, false);
            divMaskForDropdownAccountMenu.style.visibility = 'visible';
            IsVisibleAccountMenu = true;
        } else {
            window.removeEventListener('click', varBodyDocumentClickListener, false);
            divMaskForDropdownAccountMenu.style.visibility = 'hidden';
            IsVisibleAccountMenu = false;
        }
        classDropdownAccountMenuContent[0].classList.toggle("display-dropdown-account-menu");
        classDropdownAccountMenuContent[1].classList.toggle("display-dropdown-account-menu");
    }

    var varBodyDocumentClickListener = function BodyDocumentClickListener(event) {
        if (document.querySelector('#AccountMenuOptionsForDesktop').contains(event.target)
            || document.querySelector('#AccountMenuOptionsForMobile').contains(event.target)) {
            //console.log('clicked inside');
        } else {
            IsVisibleAccountMenu = false;
            divMaskForDropdownAccountMenu.style.visibility = 'hidden';
            classDropdownAccountMenuContent[0].classList.toggle("display-dropdown-account-menu");
            classDropdownAccountMenuContent[1].classList.toggle("display-dropdown-account-menu");
            window.removeEventListener('click', varBodyDocumentClickListener, false);
            //console.log('clicked outside');
        }
    }

    function ShowMobileSearch() {
        $('#tbSearch').val('');
        $('#divHeaderMobile').fadeOut(200);
        $('#divHeaderMobileSearch').fadeIn(200);
        $('#tbSearch').focus();
    }
    function HideMobileSearch() {
        $('#divHeaderMobile').fadeIn(200);
        $('#divHeaderMobileSearch').fadeOut(200);
    }
</script>
<script type="text/javascript">
        // Set divs RTL
        function SetLtrBlockScroll(className) {
            var elements = document.getElementsByClassName(className);
            for (var i = 0; i < elements.length; i++)
                elements[i].scrollLeft = elements[i].scrollWidth - elements[i].clientWidth;
        }
    </script>

<script type="text/javascript">
    $(document).ready(function () {
        $(document.body).on('click', '.c-exp,.cc-exp,.c-clp,.cc-clp', function () {
            var child = $(this).parent('div').parent('li').children('div.cl-child');
            if (child.length > 0) {
                var currentClass = $(this).attr('class');
                var newClass;
                if (currentClass.indexOf('exp') > 0) {
                    $(child).slideDown(200);
                    newClass = $(this).attr('class').replace('exp', 'clp');
                    $(this).removeClass(currentClass);
                    $(this).addClass(newClass);
                    if (currentClass.indexOf('cc') == -1)
                        $(this).parent('div').addClass('crm-item-expanded');
                } else {
                    $(child).slideUp(200);
                    newClass = $(this).attr('class').replace('clp', 'exp');
                    $(this).removeClass(currentClass);
                    $(this).addClass(newClass);
                    if (currentClass.indexOf('cc') == -1)
                        $(this).parent('div').removeClass('crm-item-expanded');
                }
            }
        });
    });
</script>

<script>
            // Quick link navigation
            if (/Android|webOS|iPhone|iPad|iPod|BlackBerry|IEMobile|Opera Mini/i.test(navigator.userAgent)) {
                $('.ql-nav').css({ 'margin': ' 5px 0', 'overflow-x': ' scroll','height': ' auto', 'padding': ' 10px 2px' });
                //$('.ql-nav li:last-child').css({ 'margin-right': ' 10px' });
                $('.ql-nav').css({ 'margin': '0 0' });
                //$('.category-books .ql-nav').css({ 'margin': '0 -10px' });
                $('.ql-nav li h2').css({ 'padding': ' 0 10px' });
                $('.ql-nav-content').css({ 'position': 'initial', 'right': 'initial', 'padding': ' 0' });
                $('.ql-nav-scroll-to-right').css({ 'display': ' none' });
                $('.ql-nav-scroll-to-left').css({ 'display': ' none' });
            } else {
                $(window).resize(function () {
                    if ($('.ql-nav-content').width() > $('.ql-nav').width())
                        $('.ql-nav-scroll-to-left').fadeIn();
                    else
                        $('.ql-nav-scroll-to-left').fadeOut();
                });

                $(document).ready(function () {
                    if ($('.ql-nav-content').width() < $('.ql-nav').width()) {
                        $('.ql-nav-scroll-to-left').css('display', 'none');
                    }
                    $('.eab').on('click', function () {
                        var contentWidhh = $('.ql-nav-content').width();
                        var easyAccessBooks = $('.ql-nav').width();
                        //Hidden part of the ql-nav-content div
                        var hiddenPart = contentWidhh - easyAccessBooks;
                        var rightPosition = $('.ql-nav-content').css('right');
                        rightPosition = rightPosition.replace('px', '');
                        var id = $(this).attr('id');

                        if (id == 'eab_left') {
                            $('.ql-nav-scroll-to-right').fadeIn();
                            var value = rightPosition - (easyAccessBooks / 2);
                            if (hiddenPart < (rightPosition - (easyAccessBooks / 2)) * -1) {
                                value = hiddenPart * -1;
                                $('.ql-nav-scroll-to-right').fadeIn();
                                $('.ql-nav-scroll-to-left').delay(900).fadeOut('fast');
                            }
                            $('.ql-nav-content').css('right', value);
                        } else {
                            $('.ql-nav-scroll-to-left').fadeIn();
                            var value2 = $('.ql-nav-content').css('right').replace('px', '');
                            value2 = Math.round(value2);
                            value2 = value2 + (easyAccessBooks / 2);
                            if (value2 > 0) {
                                value2 = 2;
                                $('.ql-nav-scroll-to-right').delay(900).fadeOut('fast');
                                $('.ql-nav-scroll-to-left').delay(1000).fadeIn();
                            }
                            $('.ql-nav-content').css('right', value2);
                        }
                    });
                });
            }
            SetLtrBlockScroll('ql-nav');
        </script>

<script type="text/javascript">
    var fullScreenPopup = document.getElementById('FullScreenPopup');
    var popupContent = document.getElementById("FullScreenPopupContent");
    var popupContentName;
    var popopMask = document.getElementById('PopupMask');

    function ShowFullScreenPopup(data) {
        popupContentName = data.PopupContent;
        popupContent.innerHTML = "";

        if (window.innerWidth >= 768)
            fullScreenPopup.style.height = '80%';
        else
            fullScreenPopup.style.height = '100%';

        switch (popupContentName) {
        case "AudioBookTableOfContents":
            popupContent.innerHTML = '<div class="full-screen-popup-loading"><div class="spinner"></div></div>';
            setFullScreenPopupBlockAndVisible();
            var params = {
                act: 'get-audio-book-table-of-contents',
                bookid: data.BookId
            }
            JavaScriptHttpRequest('/ajax/default.aspx',
                params,
                function callback(result) {
                    popupContent.innerHTML = "";
                    popupContent.insertAdjacentHTML('afterbegin', result);
                    setFullScreenPopupHeight();
                }
            );
            break;
        case "Share":
            popupContent.innerHTML = "";
            setFullScreenPopupBlockAndVisible();
            popupContent.insertAdjacentHTML('afterbegin', decodeURIComponent(document.getElementById("hfShare").value));
            setFullScreenPopupHeight();
            break;
        case "InstallApps":
            popupContent.innerHTML = "";
            setFullScreenPopupBlockAndVisible();
            popupContent.insertAdjacentHTML('afterbegin',
                decodeURIComponent(document.getElementById("hfInstallApps").value));
            setFullScreenPopupHeight();
            break;
        case "MobileNavigation":
            popupContent.innerHTML = "";
            setFullScreenPopupBlockAndVisible();
            popupContent.insertAdjacentHTML('afterbegin',
                decodeURIComponent(document.getElementById("hfMobileNavigation").value));
            setFullScreenPopupHeight();
            break;
        case "AudioBookSamples":
            popupContent.innerHTML = '<div class="full-screen-popup-loading"><div class="spinner"></div></div>';
            setFullScreenPopupBlockAndVisible();
            params = {
                act: 'get-audio-book-samples',
                bookid: data.BookId
            }
            JavaScriptHttpRequest('/ajax/default.aspx',
                params,
                function callback(result) {
                    popupContent.innerHTML = "";
                    popupContent.insertAdjacentHTML('afterbegin', result);
                    setFullScreenPopupHeight();
                }
            );
            break;
        }
        document.getElementById("FullScreenPopupTitle").innerHTML = data.Title + '<span class="line-end"></span>';

        if (window.innerWidth >= 768) {
            popopMask.style.opacity = "0.25";
            popopMask.style.visibility = "visible";
        }

        var hash = window.location.hash.substring(1);
        if (hash === popupContentName)
            window.history.replaceState(undefined, document.title, window.location.pathname);
        window.history.pushState("", document.title, "#" + popupContentName);
    }

    function setFullScreenPopupHeight() {
        var popupContentHeight = popupContent.offsetHeight;
        console.log(popupContentHeight);
        if (popupContentHeight < 550 && window.innerWidth >= 768)
            fullScreenPopup.style.height = popupContentHeight + 70 + 'px';
    }

    function setFullScreenPopupBlockAndVisible() {
        fullScreenPopup.style.display = "block";
        fullScreenPopup.style.opacity = '1';
    }

    function CloseFullScreenPopup() {

        fullScreenPopup.style.opacity = '0';
        fullScreenPopup.style.display = "none";

        popopMask.style.opacity = "0";
        popopMask.style.visibility = "hidden";

        var hash = window.location.hash.substring(1);
        if (hash === popupContentName)
            history.back();

        // Pause audiobook if audio is playing when popup closed
        var popupAudiobookSamples = document.getElementById('PopupAudiobookSamples');
        if (typeof popupAudiobookSamples !== 'undefined' && popupAudiobookSamples != null) {
            if (!popupAudiobookSamples.paused)
                popupAudiobookSamples.pause();
        }
    }

    window.onpopstate = function (event) {
        var hash = window.location.hash.substring(1);
        if (hash === '')
            CloseFullScreenPopup();
    };

</script>
</body></html>
